### [1] [ReLoop-UME: Recurrent Depth with Learnable Retrieval Registers for Universal Multimodal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28751)
*Shijie Wang,Xiangzhao Hao,Yueti Li,Guangyu Cao,Xinyu Tang,Haiyun Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLoop-UME，一种沿模型深度扩展计算而非扩张token工作区的通用多模态嵌入方法。通过分析独立训练UME模型中各层的正负样本相似度分离度，发现早期层负责多模态上下文、中后期层形成检索判别特征、最终层映射到嵌入空间。ReLoop-UME据此设计：早层只执行一次，中间检索形成块以参数共享方式循环复用，最后映射层在最后一次循环后执行。可学习的检索寄存器（Retrieval Registers）跨循环累积和交换检索特定状态，最终寄存器作为嵌入读出。在MMEB-V2和MRMR上提升检索性能，比UME-R1快44.9倍、比PLUME快1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用多模态嵌入（UME）模型要么单次前向编码，要么通过显式理由token或潜在自回归状态扩展计算。虽然后者的token扩展能改善复杂匹配，但串行生成增加了检索延迟，且最终嵌入依赖于生成的中间状态。作者提出疑问：能否在保持token工作区固定的前提下，沿模型深度扩展有用计算？

Method: 1) 对独立训练的UME模型进行逐层正负样本相似度分离分析，发现三段式进展：早期层上下文化、中后期层形成检索判别特征、最后层映射嵌入空间。2) 提出ReLoop-UME：早层执行一次，参数共享的检索形成块（retrieval-forming block）循环复用，最后映射层在最后一次循环后执行。3) 引入可学习检索寄存器（Learnable Retrieval Registers），提供跨循环累积和交换证据的持久检索特定状态，最终寄存器作为嵌入读出。

Result: 在MMEB-V2和MRMR基准上，ReLoop-UME在不同骨干网络上始终提升检索性能，同时运行速度比UME-R1快44.9倍、比PLUME快1.5倍。

Conclusion: 通过沿深度而非宽度（token数量）扩展计算，可以在不增加检索延迟的情况下获得更好的检索性能。ReLoop-UME证明了循环复用共享的检索形成块配合可学习寄存器的有效性，为设计高效的通用多模态嵌入模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedding (UME) maps heterogeneous multimodal inputs into a shared embedding space. Existing UME models either form embeddings through single forward encoding or add computation through explicit rationale tokens and latent autoregressive states. Although token expansion can improve complex matching, serial generation increases retrieval latency and makes the final embedding depend on generated intermediate states. This raises a different question: can useful computation be expanded along model depth while keeping the token workspace fixed? We analyze positive-negative similarity separation at every layer of independently trained UME models and observe a shared progression: early layers contextualize multimodal inputs, a contiguous middle-to-late stage forms retrieval-discriminative features, and the final layers map them into the embedding space. Based on this finding, we propose ReLoop-UME, which executes the early layers once, recurrently reuses a parameter-shared retrieval-forming block, and applies the final mapping layers after the last loop. Learnable Retrieval Registers provide persistent retrieval-specific states that accumulate and exchange evidence across loops, with the final register serving as the embedding readout. On MMEB-V2 and MRMR, ReLoop-UME consistently improves retrieval across different backbones while running 44.9x faster than UME-R1 and 1.5x faster than PLUME.

</details>


### [2] [SCMA: Structure-Conditioned and Metal-Aware Flow Matching for CT Metal Artifact Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28759)
*Heran Wang,Jianing Sun,Xu Jiang,Genwei Ma,Xing Zhao,Jigang Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCMA，一种结构条件化和金属感知的Flow Matching框架，用于CT金属伪影去除。该方法通过将线性插值校正图像作为样本特定结构条件、利用金属掩膜及其距离变换生成时变空间权重、以及在推理过程中交替进行投影一致性校正，实现了优于现有MAR方法的伪影抑制和结构保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT中金属物体导致束硬化、光子匮乏和散射等伪影，严重影响临床诊断。现有MAR方法存在局限：基于优化的方法会残留伪影或模糊结构，回归网络泛化能力差，生成模型缺乏样本特定结构引导和物理约束，可能产生解剖学上不一致的结构。标准无条件Flow Matching未利用样本特定结构和投影测量信息。

Method: 提出SCMA框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 将线性插值校正图像与中间状态一起馈入速度网络，作为样本特定结构条件以引导推理；(2) 从金属掩膜及其距离变换构造时变空间权重，引入Flow Matching损失中以强调金属区域及其周围的严重退化；(3) 推理时条件Flow Matching更新与投影一致性校正交替进行，利用金属迹线外的可靠测量约束预测。

Result: 在模拟和真实CT数据上的实验表明，SCMA相比代表性MAR方法更能有效抑制金属伪影、保留局部解剖结构、减少与投影测量不一致的幻觉样结构。

Conclusion: SCMA通过结构条件化和金属感知的Flow Matching设计，结合投影一致性约束，有效克服了现有MAR方法的局限，在伪影抑制和结构保真方面取得了更好的性能。

Abstract: In X-ray CT, metallic objects cause beam hardening, photon starvation, and scattering, leading to projection inconsistency, streaks, dark bands, and structural distortions that compromise clinical diagnosis and quantitative analysis. Existing metal artifact reduction (MAR) methods remain limited: optimization-based methods may leave residual artifacts or blur structures, regression networks may generalize poorly across scenarios, and generative models without sample-specific structural guidance and physical constraints may produce anatomically inconsistent structures. Flow Matching learns a continuous-time velocity field that deterministically transports a source distribution to a target distribution, providing a flexible MAR prior. However, standard unconditional Flow Matching does not exploit sample-specific structure, spatially nonuniform metal-induced degradation, or measured projections. To address these limitations, we propose SCMA, a structure-conditioned and metal-aware Flow Matching framework. First, a linear-interpolation-corrected image is fed into the velocity network with the intermediate state as a sample-specific structural condition, guiding inference toward artifact-free CT images while preserving anatomy. Second, time-varying spatial weights from the metal mask and its distance transform are incorporated into the Flow Matching loss to emphasize severe degradation within and around metal regions. Finally, conditional Flow Matching updates alternate with projection-consistency correction during inference, allowing reliable measurements outside metal traces to constrain predictions. Experiments on simulated and real CT data demonstrate that SCMA more effectively suppresses metal artifacts, preserves local anatomical structures, and reduces hallucination-like structures inconsistent with projection measurements than representative MAR methods.

</details>


### [3] [WaiT for the Signal: Simple Frequency-Aware Flow-Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28760)
*Krunoslav Lehman Pavasovic,Théophane Vallaeys,Stéphane Mallat,Giulio Biroli,Luke Zettlemoyer,Brian Karrer,Jakob Verbeek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaiT是一种基于小波的图像Transformer生成模型，通过将生成过程分解为粗/高频带，让高频带等待粗结构出现后再加入联合精炼，在ImageNet 512上实现像素空间FID 1.43（最大模型达1.3），同时降低50%采样计算量，并无需算法修改即可扩展至视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图像生成模型分辨率不断提高，全局一致性、局部细节和纹理保真度成为生成质量的关键维度。然而标准流匹配对所有空间频率一视同仁，忽略了自然的频率层级——高频带远早于粗结构就与纯噪声无法区分。这导致了生成质量在高分辨率下的瓶颈。

Method: 引入Wavelet-aware image Transformer（WaiT），通过无损小波变换将生成任务分解为粗带和细带两部分。高频带保持纯噪声状态，等待粗结构生成后再加入流中进行联合精炼。同时提出更严格的三轴评估协议，在原生分辨率下评估质量（因为标准FID通过激进降采样丢弃了细粒度细节）。

Result: 在ImageNet 512×512上，WaiT达到像素空间FID 1.43且在三轴上均为Pareto最优，采样计算量降低50%。最大2B模型在ImageNet 512上实现像素空间模型的新SOTA FID 1.3。纹理保真度上超越最强潜空间模型，并能无缝扩展到高分辨率OpenImages和视频生成，在Kinetics-600上以FVD 0.84取得SOTA且无需算法修改。

Conclusion: 通过利用自然频率层级将生成分解为粗/细带，让小波定义的高频带按需等待，WaiT同时在图像和视频生成上取得了领先的生成质量与计算效率，验证了利用频域结构改善生成模型的设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: As image generation models scale to ever higher resolutions, global coherence, local detail, and texture fidelity become critical axes for generation quality. However, standard flow matching treats all spatial frequencies uniformly, ignoring the natural frequency hierarchy where high-frequency bands become indistinguishable from pure noise far earlier than coarse structures. We introduce WaiT, a Wavelet-aware image Transformer that decomposes generation into coarse and fine bands via lossless wavelets. True to its name, the high-frequency bands wait for the signal: staying pure noise until coarse structure has emerged, then joining the flow for joint refinement. Since standard FID discards fine-grained detail through aggressive downsampling, we introduce a more stringent three-axis evaluation protocol to assess quality at native resolution. On ImageNet 512x512, WaiT achieves a pixel-space FID of 1.43 and is Pareto-optimal across all three axes, reducing sampling compute by up to 50%. With our largest 2B model, we set a new state-of-the-art FID of 1.3 for pixel-space models on ImageNet 512 resolution. Our formulation outperforms even the strongest latent-space models on texture fidelity, and scales seamlessly to high-resolution OpenImages and to video generation, achieving a state-of-the-art FVD of 0.84 on Kinetics-600 with no algorithmic modifications.

</details>


### [4] [Uncertainty-Aware Deepfake Detection via Multi-View Structural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28769)
*Muhammad Umar Farooq,Kutub Uddin,Awais Khan,Khalid Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于不确定性感知的深度伪造检测框架，通过整合视觉、语义和结构三种证据流，并结合分支间分歧校准（IBDC）机制，在跨分布偏移场景下实现SOTA泛化性能与更好的校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键型的生物特征识别和取证应用需要准确的预测和可靠的置信度估计，尤其是在分布偏移条件下。现有基于基础模型的深度伪造检测器对OOD操纵数据往往表现出过度自信的预测，限制了其在实际部署中的适用性。

Method: 提出三流整合的深度伪造检测框架：(1) 视觉流：基于适配的CLIP编码器；(2) 语义流：通过可微约束建模面部属性间的一致性；(3) 结构流：捕捉语义特征与取证特征之间的类依赖模式。同时引入Inter-Branch Disagreement Calibration（IBDC），一种基于分歧感知的不确定性建模机制，将预测不确定性与各证据流间的冲突关联起来。

Result: 以FaceForensics++为训练源，在多个跨数据集OOD基准上实现了SOTA泛化性能，并持续改善校准效果和选择性预测性能。

Conclusion: 将互补证据与分歧感知的不确定性相结合，为分布偏移下可信赖且良好校准的深度伪造检测提供了坚实的框架基础。

Abstract: Security-critical biometric and forensic applications require accurate predictions and reliable confidence estimates, particularly under distribution shift. This challenge is especially acute for deepfake detection, where foundation-model-based detectors often exhibit overconfident predictions on out-of-distribution manipulations, which limits their suitability for operational deployment. We propose an uncertainty-aware deepfake detection framework that identifies manipulations through inconsistencies across complementary evidence sources. The framework integrates three streams: a visual stream based on an adapted CLIP encoder, a semantic stream that models consistency among facial attributes through differentiable constraints, and a structural stream that captures class-dependent dependency patterns between semantic and forensic features. To effectively combine these signals, we introduce Inter-Branch Disagreement Calibration (IBDC), a disagreement-aware uncertainty modeling mechanism that links predictive uncertainty to conflicts among evidence streams. Extensive cross-dataset experiments using FaceForensics++ as the training source demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art generalization across multiple out-of-distribution benchmarks while consistently improving calibration and selective prediction performance. These results show that combining complementary evidence with disagreement-aware uncertainty provides a robust foundation for trustworthy and well-calibrated deepfake detection under distribution shift.

</details>


### [5] [Do Medical Foundation Models Generalize on the African Brain?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28771)
*Kaouther Mouheb,Gonzalo Esteban Mosquera Rojas,Juancito van Leeuwen,Stefan Klein,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估医学基础模型（FMs）在非洲与非非洲脑MRI数据上的泛化能力，发现FMs不表现出针对非洲人群的固有偏倚，性能差异更可能与数据集规模而非数据来源相关；分类任务收益有限，分割任务收益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型在脑MRI分析中的应用日益广泛，但其评估仍以高资源数据集为主，对非洲人群队列的泛化能力研究不足，需要系统评估FMs是否对非洲数据存在偏见。

Method: 研究在两个任务上评估FMs：使用尼日利亚数据集的痴呆分类任务和BraTS-Africa的脑肿瘤分割任务。评估了两种通用FMs（BrainIAC、3DINO）和两种分割专用FMs（MedSAM2、Medical-SAM2），并与从零训练的基线模型进行对比。

Result: 分类任务中FMs收益有限（BrainIAC最高ROC-AUC为0.86）；分割任务中FMs一致提升性能（MedSAM2最高Dice达0.86）。非洲与非非洲人群队列之间的性能差异不一致，且更可能与数据集大小而非数据来源相关。

Conclusion: 结果表明FMs并未对非洲人群表现出内在偏倚，非洲神经影像数据集的可获得性和多样性不足才是阻碍FMs稳健评估和部署的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Medical foundation models (FMs) are increasingly used for brain MRI analysis. However, their evaluation remains dominated by high-resource datasets, leaving generalization to African cohorts underexplored. We assess whether FMs generalize equally to African and non-African brain MRI data across two tasks: dementia classification using a Nigerian dataset and brain tumor segmentation using BraTS-Africa. We evaluate two generalist FMs (BrainIAC, 3DINO) and two segmentation-specific FMs (MedSAM2, Medical-SAM2) against a from-scratch baseline. For classification, FMs provide limited gains (highest ROC-AUC of 0.86 with BrainIAC), whereas for segmentation they consistently improve performance, reaching up to 0.86 Dice with MedSAM2. Performance differences between African and non-African cohorts are inconsistent and appear more related to dataset size than data origin. These results suggest that FMs do not exhibit an inherent bias against African cohorts, and highlight the limited availability and diversity of African neuroimaging datasets as the main barrier to robust evaluation and deployment.

</details>


### [6] [Can Synthetic Data Overcome the Generalization Limits of AI-Based Flower and Pod Detection Across Cowpea Breeding Genotypes and Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28796)
*Hamid Kamangir,Jonathan Berlingeri,Earl Ranario,Isaac Kazuo Uyehara,Lars Lundqvist,Heesup Yun,Christine H. Diepenbrock,Brian N. Bailey,J. Mason Earles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基因型-环境（G×E）变化对AI豆豆花与豆荚检测模型性能的影响，并提出用程序化3D合成图像替代真实标注来弥补这一泛化差距。通过域差距感知的相机逼真度增强策略和线性HDR表示优化，仅需5张真实图像即可匹配或超越真实数据基线的空间泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量表型分析需要能跨基因型、地点和生长季节泛化的AI视觉模型，但这类模型在新条件下常丢失精度。为每个G×E组合标注真实图像成本极高，作者试图量化G×E变化对检测的影响，并探索合成数据是否能替代真实标注负担。

Method: 使用程序化3D豆豆模型渲染合成图像进行监督训练；采用域差距感知的相机逼真度增强策略，通过Wasserstein距离优化以匹配实测真实图像统计；对比线性HDR表示与8-bit表示对检测增益的影响；在加州两个地点、两个季节的豆豆花和豆荚检测任务上评估，包括不同shot数量的少样本实验。

Result: 花检测mAP@50从76.3%降至最低50.6%；合成监督单独使用优于预训练但受相机成像域差距限制；优化的HDR合成数据配合仅5张真实图像即匹配或超越真实数据基线的空间泛化性能；豆荚检测在最低shot数量下获益最大，时间平移下增益较温和。

Conclusion: 合成数据可以克服AI花和豆荚检测的泛化极限，但前提是必须对域差距进行测量和优化，而非假定其不存在。域差距主要源于相机成像过程而非场景内容，HDR表示能更好地将测得的域差距转化为检测增益。

Abstract: High-throughput phenotyping requires AI-enabled computer vision models that generalize across genotypes, locations, and growing seasons, yet such models often lose accuracy under new conditions. Annotating real imagery for every genotype-by-environment (G x E) combination a breeding program encounters is prohibitively expensive. We quantify how G x E shifts affect AI-based detection of cowpea flowers and pods across two California locations and two growing seasons. Flower detection mAP@50 fell from 76.3% to as low as 50.6% under unseen shifts, and pod detection was more sensitive. Feature-space and image-quality diagnostics confirmed these losses track measurable distributional shifts. Because closing this gap with real data alone is not practical, we test whether synthetic imagery, rendered from a procedural 3D cowpea model, can substitute for that annotation burden. Synthetic supervision alone improved over pretraining but remained limited by a domain gap driven by camera image formation, not scene content. A domain-gap-aware camera-realism augmentation strategy, optimized against measured real-image statistics via Wasserstein distance, narrowed this gap, and a linear HDR representation converted a smaller measured gap into a larger detection gain than an 8-bit representation. Optimized HDR synthetic data combined with as few as five real images matched or exceeded the real-data baseline for spatial generalization, and pod detection benefited most at the lowest shot counts, with more modest gains under temporal shift. These results show that synthetic data can overcome the generalization limits of AI-based flower and pod detection, but only when the domain gap is measured and optimized rather than assumed away.

</details>


### [7] [FocusGS: Spatial Delta Layers for Local Repair and Deterministic Editing of Trained 3D Gaussian Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28834)
*Yiqun Pan,Yukun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FocusGS将3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的局部修复与确定性编辑统一为复合空间增量操作。修复是纯粹加性的特例（基础操作项为空，仅添加局部高斯基），确定性编辑则通过"擦除-插入分解"(EIF)结合旧载体擦除与新内容插入。实验表明：局部修复平均提升目标区域PSNR 7.91 dB；83次确定性编辑试验中目标ROI全部改善，平均编辑ROI PSNR达21.97 dB（增益+11.05 dB）；5个公开编辑案例下EIF方案达到33.17 dB目标掩码PSNR和0.994目标增量相关性，而文本驱动基线均无法完成规定更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS工作流聚焦于全局重建、训练时的密度控制或开放式生成编辑，缺乏对已训练资产进行精确局部维护的能力，因此需要一种轻量级、可验证的局部维修与确定性编辑统一方案。

Method: 提出FocusGS，将局部修复与确定性编辑统一表述为复合空间增量（composite spatial deltas）。修复是纯加性特例（基础操作项为空），编辑采用擦除-插入分解(EIF)将旧内容载体擦除与新内容插入相结合，并针对空间梯度饥饿问题进行专门处理。

Result: 局部修复在93个评估视角下目标区域PSNR提升7.91 dB；83个确定性编辑试验中目标ROI全部提升，平均编辑ROI PSNR为21.97 dB，平均增益+11.05 dB；5个公开编辑案例中FocusGS-EIF达到33.17 dB目标掩码PSNR和0.994目标增量相关性，且文本驱动基线均无法完成规定更新。

Conclusion: FocusGS提供了一种轻量级、可验证的3DGS维护算子，弥补了现有工作流在已训练资产局部精确维修与确定性编辑方面的空白。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is evolving from one-time reconstruction into deliverable, inspectable, and maintainable visual assets. Existing workflows focus on global reconstruction, training-time density control, or open-ended generative editing, leaving trained assets without precise local maintenance. We propose FocusGS, which unifies local repair and deterministic editing as composite spatial deltas. Repair is the purely additive special case: its base-manipulation term is empty, and it adds only local Gaussian bases; deterministic editing uses erase-insert factorization (EIF) to combine old-carrier erasure with new-content insertion. FocusGS addresses spatial gradient starvation: local repair raises target-region PSNR by 7.91 dB over 93 evaluation views. Across all 83 deterministic editing trials, the target ROI improves, with a trial-averaged mean edited ROI PSNR of 21.97 dB and a mean gain of +11.05 dB; across five public editing cases, FocusGS-EIF reaches 33.17 dB Target-mask PSNR and 0.994 Target-delta Correlation, while both text-driven baselines fail to complete the prescribed updates. FocusGS provides a lightweight, verifiable 3DGS maintenance operator.

</details>


### [8] [A Unified Benchmark of Deep Learning Models for Multi-task 3D Brain Tumor Segmentation from Magnetic Resonance Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28858)
*Diego J. Torrejón,Luna Y. Hernández,Javier Sánchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的实验基准，在完全同质的训练和评估条件下，比较五种3D分割模型（3D U-Net、SegResNet、Swin UNETR、SegMamba、SegMambaV2）在两种脑肿瘤分割数据集（BraTS 2023颅内脑膜瘤和BraTS 2024治疗后胶质瘤）上的性能，同时评估分割准确性和计算成本（推理时间、模型大小）之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量深度学习架构被提出用于脑肿瘤MRI自动分割，但不同研究往往使用不同的数据集、预处理策略、训练协议和评估流程，导致客观比较困难。因此需要一个在统一实验条件下的基准来公平对比不同架构范式的性能。

Method: 在完全相同的预处理、数据增强、优化策略和评估协议下，对五种代表性3D分割模型（包括传统CNN的3D U-Net和SegResNet、Transformer的Swin UNETR、以及状态空间模型SSM的SegMamba和SegMambaV2）在两个不同临床场景的数据集上进行训练和评估，并综合使用分割精度指标和计算成本指标（推理时间与模型大小）进行对比。

Result: 研究提供了不同架构范式在3D脑肿瘤分割任务中准确性与计算效率之间权衡的实际洞察，明确了各架构在不同临床场景下的适用性。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了不同架构范式（CNN、Transformer、SSM）在挑战性3D脑肿瘤分割任务中的表现差异，为临床应用中模型选择提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Automatic brain tumor segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) has become a fundamental task in computer-assisted diagnosis, treatment planning, and disease monitoring. Although numerous deep learning architectures have recently been proposed, objective comparisons remain challenging because published studies often employ different datasets, preprocessing strategies, training protocols, and evaluation procedures. This work presents a unified experimental benchmark for comparing representative convolutional neural networks (CNNs), Transformer-based models, and recent State Space Model (SSM) architectures under homogeneous experimental conditions. Five state-of-the-art three-dimensional segmentation models, including 3D U-Net, SegResNet, Swin UNETR, SegMamba, and SegMambaV2, are evaluated on two brain tumor segmentation datasets representing distinct clinical scenarios: intracranial meningioma segmentation (BraTS 2023) and post-treatment glioma segmentation (BraTS 2024). All architectures are trained using identical preprocessing, data augmentation, optimization strategies, and evaluation protocols to ensure a fair comparison. Performance is assessed using segmentation accuracy metrics together with computational cost indicators, including inference time and the size of each model. The results provide practical insights into the trade-offs between segmentation accuracy and computational efficiency, highlighting the suitability of different architectural paradigms for challenging three-dimensional brain tumor segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [9] [Physics-Aligned Self-Supervised Learning for Scientific Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28868)
*Bashir Kazimi,Stefan Sandfeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向科学成像模态的自监督学习（SSL）数据增强设计的原理性流程，强调数据增强应遵循物理测量过程的对称性与采集约束，而非盲目移植自然图像的增强管线。作者以电子显微镜（实空间显微与倒易空间4D-STEM衍射）为实例，在五种SSL范式（DINOv2、SimCLR、MAE、VICRegL、I-JEPA）上验证了物理对齐增强在分类与晶体取向回归任务中的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准数据增强管线是为自然图像设计的，而科学成像模态由物理测量过程支配，具有不同的对称性和采集约束。盲目强制与物理约束矛盾的增强不变性会扭曲学习表示并限制下游性能。当前从机器学习转向新科学模态的从业者缺乏设计增强的指导原则，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一个可复现的增强设计流程，包含四个步骤：(1) 枚举候选增强；(2) 通过测量算子为每个增强标注物理标签；(3) 使用表示几何诊断进行验证；(4) 通过单因素消融确认增强的必要性。形式上，物理对齐的增强集合被定义为测量一致对称性与采集驱动扰动的并集。该方法被实例化应用于实空间电子显微镜和倒易空间4D-STEM衍射，并在五种SSL框架上系统评估。

Result: 物理对齐增强在依赖跨视图一致性的SSL目标上显著提升下游性能，降低晶体取向回归的测地线误差，并在真实采集变异性（探测器增益、分辨率损失）下提高鲁棒性，同时系统地重塑表示几何结构。实验覆盖分类与回归两类任务，跨五种主流SSL范式一致验证。

Conclusion: 增强设计应被视为科学自监督学习中一个主要的、可控的归纳偏置来源。该流程是模态无关的，可推广到医学影像、遥感成像等其他测量驱动领域。作者将物理知识与SSL框架结合，为科学应用提供了一套系统化、可验证的增强选择方法论。

Abstract: Data augmentations define the invariances learned by self-supervised learning (SSL). Standard augmentation pipelines were designed for natural images, yet scientific imaging modalities are governed by physical measurement processes with distinct symmetry and acquisition constraints. Enforcing invariances that contradict these constraints can distort learned representations and limit downstream performance, but practitioners moving from machine learning into a new scientific modality currently have little guidance beyond transferring natural-image pipelines unexamined. We address this gap with a principled, reproducible procedure for augmentation design in scientific SSL: we formalise the physics-aligned augmentation set as a union of measurement-consistent symmetries and acquisition-driven perturbations, and we give a concrete, largely label-free workflow---enumerate candidates, label each by the measurement operator, validate with representation-geometry diagnostics, and confirm by single-factor ablation---for selecting them. We instantiate the procedure for real-space electron microscopy and reciprocal-space 4D-STEM diffraction, and evaluate it across five SSL paradigms (DINOv2, SimCLR, MAE, VICRegL, I-JEPA) on classification and crystal-orientation regression. Physics-aligned augmentations substantially improve downstream performance for objectives relying on cross-view consistency, reduce geodesic error and improve robustness under realistic acquisition variability (detector gain, resolution loss), and systematically reshape representation geometry. While our experiments use electron microscopy, the procedure is modality-agnostic and applies to other measurement-driven domains such as medical and remote-sensing imaging. These results position augmentation design as a primary, and controllable, source of inductive bias in scientific self-supervised learning.

</details>


### [10] [Group-wise Supervision with Focal-Dice Loss for Long-Tailed Indoor Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28935)
*Qi Zheng,Zihuang Su,Xiao Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Group-UFD Occ方法，针对室内场景3D语义占用预测中物体类别严重长尾分布的问题，通过分层语义监督和协同损失优化来提升模型在尾类类别上的性能，在EmbodiedScan数据集上相对基线提升11.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内3D语义占用预测面临物体类别多样性高、存在严重长尾分布的问题，这与结构化室外环境不同，成为限制现有模型性能的核心瓶颈。

Method: 该方法包含两个层面的创新：(1) 架构层面：引入细粒度语义分组策略，设计多尺度、并行的"主-专家"预测头，通过深度正则化引导模型高效学习尾类特征；(2) 优化层面：引入Unified Focal-Dice (UFD)损失，该协同损失函数在体素级别动态关注困难样本，同时从区域角度优化预测物体的几何完整性。

Result: 在大规模EmbodiedScan数据集上的实验表明，该方法相对基线取得11.38%的相对提升，在多个关键长尾类别上获得显著的精度增益。

Conclusion: 通过分层语义监督与协同损失优化的结合，Group-UFD Occ有效缓解了室内场景3D语义占用预测中的长尾分布问题，验证了语义分组策略和UFD损失的联合有效性。

Abstract: Recently, 3D semantic occupancy prediction has garnered increasing attention for understanding the indoor scene. However, unlike structured outdoor environments, indoor scenes feature a high diversity of object categories that exhibit a severe long-tailed distribution, which has become a core bottleneck limiting the performance of existing models. To tackle this challenge, we propose a novel method, Group-UFD Occ, based on hierarchical semantic supervision and synergistic loss optimization. At the architectural level, we introduce a fine-grained semantic grouping strategy and design multi-scale, parallel ``main-expert'' prediction heads to guide the model in efficiently learning tail-class features through deep regularization. At the optimization level, we introduce the Unified Focal-Dice (UFD) loss. This synergistic loss function dynamically focuses on hard samples at the per-voxel level. Meanwhile, it simultaneously optimizes the geometric integrity of predicted objects from a region-based perspective. We conducted experiments on the large-scale EmbodiedScan dataset. The results demonstrate that our method yields a relative improvement of 11.38\% over the baseline, with substantial accuracy gains in several critical long-tailed categories.

</details>


### [11] [DiffAttack: Evasion Attacks Against Face Recognition via Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28936)
*Omid Ahmadieh,Nima Karimian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffAttack，一种基于潜在扩散模型的对抗性人脸生成方法，通过潜在空间优化引导生成朝向目标身份嵌入，在多个面部识别模型上实现84.86%的平均攻击成功率，并显著优于传统噪声和语义方法的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对人脸生物识别系统的对抗攻击方法存在性能不足、生成图像质量不高难以不被人类察觉，以及在源图像和目标图像属于不同人口统计群体或性别时容易失效等问题，亟需一种更强大的对抗人脸生成方法。

Method: DiffAttack利用潜在扩散模型（LDM）作为生成器，直接在潜在空间中优化，以人脸识别模型测量的目标身份嵌入为引导信号，驱动生成过程朝向目标身份方向演化，从而生成高保真且具有较强欺骗性的对抗人脸图像。

Result: 在FFHQ和CelebA-HQ标准基准上，DiffAttack在多个面部识别模型（如FaceNet）上取得84.86%的平均攻击成功率，迁移性上超越传统基于噪声的方法15.28%，超越语义方法约5.21%。

Conclusion: DiffAttack通过潜在空间优化和扩散模型引导，实现了更高攻击成功率与更强迁移性的对抗人脸生成，同时保持了良好的图像质量，为面部识别系统的安全性评估提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Facial biometric identification relies on the distinctiveness of user attributes within a high-dimensional embedding space. However, the decision boundaries of deep face recognition (FR) systems are often sufficiently narrow that they can be conflated, rendering the models vulnerable to adversarial attacks. In such scenarios, the FR system fails to distinguish between an authentic source and a meticulously crafted adversarial face. Existing adversarial methods targeting facial biometrics are limited in both performance and their ability to generate high-quality images that are imperceptible to humans. Moreover, these methods often fail when the source and target images belong to different demographic groups or genders. To address these limitations, we present a novel approach for adversarial face generation via latent-space optimization. We leverage latent diffusion models directly to guide generation toward target identity embeddings, as measured by a face recognition model. Our proposed \textbf{DiffAttack} framework has been evaluated on standard benchmarks, such as the FFHQ and CelebA-HQ datasets. DiffAttack significantly outperforms existing adversarial techniques, achieving a high average attack success rate of 84.86% across multiple face recognition models (e.g., FaceNet). Notably, DiffAttack demonstrates superior transferability, surpassing traditional noise-based methods by over 15.28% and semantic-based approaches by approximately 5.21% on benchmark datasets like FFHQ and CelebA-HQ.

</details>


### [12] [Automated classification method of COVID-19 cases from chest CT volumes using 2D and 3D hybrid CNN for anisotropic volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28950)
*Masahiro Oda,Tong Zheng,Yuichiro Hayashi,Yoshito Otake,Masahiro Hashimoto,Toshiaki Akashi,Shigeki Aoki,Kensaku Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于2D/3D混合特征提取流的卷积神经网络（CNN），用于胸部CT体积的COVID-19疑似病例自动分类，在包含1288个CT体积的数据集上取得了83.3%的平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19全球大流行导致感染者和死亡人数激增，医疗机构的医务人员出现短缺。计算机辅助诊断（CAD）系统可以提供快速、定量的诊断结果，有助于缓解人员短缺问题，因此需要开发高效的COVID-19自动分类方法。

Method: 提出一种COVID-19分类卷积神经网络（CNN），采用2D/3D混合特征提取流。该特征提取流在CT体积中三个相互垂直的平面上提取图像特征，然后将这些特征进行融合以进行分类，旨在有效提取胸部CT体积等各向异性体积中的图像特征。

Result: 使用包含1288个CT体积的数据集评估了所提方法的分类准确率，平均分类准确率为83.3%，高于不具有2D和3D混合特征提取流的分类CNN。

Conclusion: 所提出的2D/3D混合特征提取流CNN在胸部CT体积的COVID-19分类任务上表现优于传统无混合特征提取流的CNN，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes an automated classification method of chest CT volumes based on likelihood of COVID-19 cases. Novel coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) spreads over the world, causing a large number of infected patients and deaths. Sudden increase in the number of COVID-19 patients causes a manpower shortage in medical institutions. Computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) system provides quick and quantitative diagnosis results. CAD system for COVID-19 enables efficient diagnosis workflow and contributes to reduce such manpower shortage. This paper proposes an automated classification method of chest CT volumes for COVID-19 diagnosis assistance. We propose a COVID-19 classification convolutional neural network (CNN) that has a 2D/3D hybrid feature extraction flows. The 2D/3D hybrid feature extraction flows are designed to effectively extract image features from anisotropic volumes such as chest CT volumes for diagnosis. The flows extract image features on three mutually perpendicular planes in CT volumes and then combine the features to perform classification. Classification accuracy of the proposed method was evaluated using a dataset that contains 1288 CT volumes. An averaged classification accuracy was 83.3%. The accuracy was higher than that of a classification CNN which does not have 2D and 3D hybrid feature extraction flows.

</details>


### [13] [Retrieval-Driven Training-Free AI-Generated Video Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28955)
*Renxi Cheng,Chaolei Han,Jie Gui,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的AI生成视频溯源方法，将视频归因形式化为实例检索任务，通过生成指纹管线（包含正交颜色变换、多尺度量化残差生成和时间-语义聚合）捕获生成模型在视频帧中留下的伪影，在GenVidBench基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频越来越逼真，难以与真实视频区分，对网络安全和社会治理构成威胁。现有视觉归因方法主要针对图像，依赖图像生成模型，缺乏对大规模AI生成视频数据的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种训练免费的AI生成视频归因范式。将视频归因形式化为实例检索任务，设计基于生成指纹的管线，包含三部分：自适应的正交颜色变换、多尺度量化残差生成、时间-语义聚合，逐步捕获和整合生成模型在视频帧中引入的伪影。

Result: 在GenVidBench基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在AI生成视频检测和归因任务中均表现优异，Rank-1准确率达到20.5%，平均精确率均值(mAP)达到16.6%，超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该训练免费的生成指纹方法能有效实现AI生成视频的检测与溯源，为视频取证和法规监管提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: AI-generated videos are becoming increasingly realistic and difficult to distinguish from authentic ones, which facilitates malicious misuse and poses growing threats to cybersecurity and social governance. Attributing AI-generated videos to their specific generative sources is therefore of critical importance for forensic investigation and legal regulation. However, most existing visual attribution methods focus on images and particularly rely on the image generation model, thereby lacking the ability to generalize to large-scale AI-generated video data. To address these limitations, we introduce an training-free AI-generated video attribution paradigm. Specifically, we formulates AI-generated video attribution as an instance retrieval task, and design a generative fingerprint-based pipeline. This pipeline consists of an adapted orthogonal color transformation, multi-scale quantized residual generation, and temporal-semantic aggregation, progressively capturing and integrating artifacts introduced by generative models across video frames. Extensive experiments on the GenVidBench benchmark demonstrate that our method achieves strong performance in both AI-generated video detection and attribution, outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods with a Rank-1 accuracy of 20.5% and a mean Average Precision of 16.6%. The code is at https://github.com/renxi-seu/Video_Attribution.

</details>


### [14] [Visual Distribution Anchoring for Efficient Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28967)
*Pouya Parsa,Raoof Zare Moayedi,Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VDA（Visual Distribution Anchoring），一种无需训练的视觉-语言模型目标域适配框架。它利用离线估计的类级视觉原型来增强冻结的语义分类器，无需目标标签、目标侧优化、迭代细化或测试查询访问，即可实现固定的可缓存分类器。在十个ImageNet到目标域的迁移任务中，该方法一致提升了零样本CLIP、TCP和MaPLe的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示调优方法在效率与适配能力之间存在权衡：静态文本提示容易过拟合源域类别，图像条件提示增加了每实例的计算开销，多模态调优则修改了视觉分支。作者希望在不牺牲效率的前提下实现有效的目标域适配，从而提出了一个无需训练的框架。

Method: VDA使用冻结的语义分类器和域模板分类器将无标签目标图像划分为类相关分组，然后通过置信度排序的图像特征形成归一化原型，并与语义分类器使用一个全局权重进行融合。框架的核心是类级视觉原型（由文本到质心映射器无法在分布偏移下合成，因此需要从无标签目标池中估计）。

Result: 在十个ImageNet到目标域的迁移任务中，同一冻结设计使零样本CLIP、TCP和MaPLe分别提升3.22、3.39和3.35个百分点，每个设置下十个目标中有九个得到改善。视觉修正进一步将无泄漏的PromptKD提升2.79个百分点。对照实验表明类特定分组是性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: VDA验证了类级视觉锚定作为一种无需训练的目标域适配范式的有效性。它能够补充多种现有分类器（零样本、源提示、多模态提示和目标蒸馏），并表明即使是类不正确的局部伪标签误差在目标域中仍可具有实用价值。

Abstract: Prompt tuning adapts vision--language models with few trainable parameters, but existing approaches trade off efficiency and adaptation: static textual prompts can overfit source classes, image-conditioned prompts add per-instance computation, and multimodal tuning modifies the visual branch. We propose VDA (Visual Distribution Anchoring), a training-free target adaptation framework that augments a frozen semantic classifier with class-level visual prototypes estimated offline from an unlabeled target pool. We first ask whether prototypes can be synthesized from class names. A text-to-centroid mapper reconstructs held-out source prototypes but fails under dataset shift because class names specify semantic identity, not target-domain appearance. An oracle analysis confirms that true target prototypes are highly discriminative. VDA therefore uses frozen semantic and domain-template classifiers to partition unlabeled target images into class-correlated groups. Confidence-ranked image features form normalized prototypes, fused with the semantic classifier using one global weight. Adaptation requires no target labels, target-side optimization, uniform class-prior assumption, iterative refinement, or test-query access, and yields a fixed, cacheable classifier. Controlled experiments show that class-specific partitioning drives gains and that visually local pseudo-label errors can remain useful despite being class-incorrect. Across ten ImageNet-to-target transfers, the same frozen design improves zero-shot CLIP, TCP, and MaPLe by 3.22, 3.39, and 3.35 points, respectively, improving nine of ten targets in every setting. Its visual correction further improves leakage-free PromptKD by 2.79 points, complementing zero-shot, source-prompted, multimodal-prompted, and target-distilled classifiers.

</details>


### [15] [SafeNexus: Discovering and Steering Modality-Universal Safety Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28969)
*Jian Yu,Fei Shen,Cong Wang,Jian Wang,Lu Jin. Xiaoyu Du,Jinhui Tang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeNexus，一个跨模态安全对齐框架，通过神经元级别的干预策略来弥补多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在跨模态安全防护上的不足。该框架首先定位模态绑定的安全神经元（BS-Neurons）和模态通用的安全神经元（US-Neurons），然后通过激活级安全放大器和安全神经元校准器两种策略增强模型安全性，同时保持其整体效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）虽然已展现出良好的安全性能，但扩展到多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）后，扩展的多模态能力与现有安全机制之间存在显著差距。当前的安全防御主要局限于特定模态设置，缺乏对跨模态威胁的鲁棒性。因此，需要一种能够弥合跨模态安全漏洞的新框架。

Method: SafeNexus框架包含三个核心组成部分：（1）神经元定位范式：通过表征中间层激活模式并使用重要性评分量化功能显著性来识别功能特化神经元；（2）利用对比数据识别模态绑定安全神经元（BS-Neurons），并通过定向抑制验证其在各模态内调节安全行为的作用；进一步通过跨模态分析将模态通用的安全神经元（US-Neurons）定义为各模态BS-Neurons的共享子集；（3）提出两种安全对齐策略：激活级安全放大器（增强US-Neurons的激活幅度）和安全神经元校准器（通过定向微调校准US-Neurons）。

Result: 实验表明，抑制US-Neurons会显著降低模型在多个模态上的安全性能，同时几乎不影响整体效用。在涵盖多种模态组合的安全基准测试中，SafeNexus的性能优于当前最先进的方法，同时有效保持了模型的实用性。

Conclusion: SafeNexus通过识别和干预模态通用的安全神经元，为多模态大语言模型提供了一种有效的跨模态安全对齐方案。该方法揭示了安全神经元在跨模态防御中的核心作用，并通过放大器与校准器两种互补策略在不损害模型效用的前提下显著提升了安全性。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising safety performance, extending them to Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exposes a significant gap between expanded multimodal capabilities and existing safety mechanisms. Current defenses remain predominantly confined to specific modal settings, thereby limiting their robustness against broader cross-modal threats. To bridge this gap, we introduce SafeNexus, a cross-modal safety alignment framework that adopts a dedicated neuron-level intervention strategy. First, we formulate a neuron localization paradigm that identifies functionally specialized neurons by characterizing intermediate-layer activation patterns and quantifying their functional salience through importance scoring. Building upon this paradigm, we exploit contrastive data to identify modality-bound safety neurons (BS-Neurons), and validate their role in regulating safety behavior within each modality via targeted suppression. Further cross-modal analysis defines modality-universal safety neurons (US-Neurons) as the shared subset of BS-Neurons identified across individual modalities, serving as the core for defending against harmful cross-modal attacks. We observe that suppressing these neurons substantially degrades safety performance across modalities, while leaving overall utility largely unaffected. Building on these insights, we propose two safety alignment strategies: activation-level safety amplifier and safety neuron calibrator. The proposed strategies enhance model safety through two distinct routes: the former amplifies the activation magnitudes of US-Neurons, while the latter selectively calibrates them via targeted fine-tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms prevailing state-of-the-art approaches on safety benchmarks spanning diverse modality combinations, while effectively preserving utility.

</details>


### [16] [LegoQ: Density-Matrix Representation Learning with Spectral-Spatial State Transitions for Hyperspectral Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28970)
*Weijia Cao,Xiaofei Yang,Fu Wang,Yicong Zhou,Xiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高光谱图像分类的经典密度矩阵表示学习框架（\method）。该方法将光谱波段分组映射为半正定、厄米、迹归一化的矩阵状态，通过谱域、空间域和组间转移的堆叠层更新状态，并使用Uhlmann保真度与可学习的类原型密度矩阵比较。在Indian Pines和WHU-Hi-LongKou数据集上取得了优异的分类精度，同时提供了样本级诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱分类器将像素或块编码为确定性向量并应用softmax头，虽然判别有效，但无法直接揭示样本的混合程度或不确定性。高光谱图像本身存在混合像元、光谱模糊、类别不平衡和标注有限等问题，需要一种能显式暴露样本混合/不确定性的表示方法。

Method: 将光谱波段划分为多个组，每组映射为一个正半定、厄米、迹归一化的密度矩阵状态。构建可组合的谱域、空间域和组间转移层堆叠来更新状态，并反复将状态投影回有效状态集合。最终聚合各组状态，通过与可学习的类原型密度矩阵计算Uhlmann保真度进行分类，避免了特征展平操作。此外，归一化特征谱、von Neumann熵、纯度和原型保真度提供了样本级诊断信息。

Result: 在Indian Pines上，十次运行的总体精度达96.20±0.70%，平均精度95.57±1.29%，kappa系数95.66±0.80%；在WHU-Hi-LongKou上，十次运行的最佳总体精度达97.52%。分类图和特征投影显示转移堆叠产生了更紧凑且分离度更好的类结构。

Conclusion: 受约束的矩阵状态学习是高光谱分类中纯向量方法的一种实用替代方案，无需量子硬件即可实现，且能提供更丰富的样本级诊断能力。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image classification is complicated by mixed pixels, spectral ambiguity, class imbalance, and limited annotations. Most current classifiers encode a pixel or patch as a deterministic vector and apply a linear or multilayer softmax head. Although effective for discrimination, this representation does not directly expose how mixed or uncertain a sample is. This paper presents \method, a classical density-matrix representation learning framework for hyperspectral images. The spectral bands are divided into groups and each group is mapped to a positive semi-definite, Hermitian, trace-normalized matrix state. A composable stack of spectral, spatial, and inter-group transitions then updates the states while repeatedly projecting them back to the valid state set. Instead of flattening the final features, \method\ aggregates the group states and compares them with learnable class-prototype density matrices through Uhlmann fidelity. The normalized eigenspectrum, von Neumann entropy, purity, and prototype fidelity provide sample-level diagnostics that are unavailable from a conventional vector head. On Indian Pines, ten runs yield an overall accuracy of $96.20\pm0.70\%$, an average accuracy of $95.57\pm1.29\%$, and a kappa coefficient of $95.66\pm0.80\%$. On WHU-Hi-LongKou, the best of ten runs reaches $97.52\%$ overall accuracy. Classification maps and feature projections show that the transition stack produces compact and better separated class structures. The results support constrained matrix-state learning as a practical alternative to vector-only hyperspectral classification without requiring quantum hardware.

</details>


### [17] [RAID: Towards Robust AI-Generated Image Detection with Bit-Reversed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28974)
*Renxi Cheng,Jie Gui,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于位平面（bit-plane）的AI生成图像检测方法，通过构建位反转图像（bit-reversed image）、梯度驱动的补丁选择和卷积分类器组成的简洁高效流水线，并从数学角度提供了理论有效性分析。该方法在40多个基准上超越现有方法，且速度比同类方法快近100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成模型的快速发展使得AI生成图像与真实图像难以区分，可能存在滥用风险。现有检测方法忽视了真实图像与虚假图像之间的本质差异，缺乏鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出一个简洁有效的流水线，包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建位反转图像，创新性地从位平面角度研究AI生成图像检测；(2) 基于梯度的补丁选择策略；(3) 卷积分类器。同时从数学角度提供了理论分析来证明方法的有效性，并引入了两个具有挑战性的数据集用于评估。

Result: 在超过40个基准上优于现有方法，涵盖跨生成器泛化、跨数据集泛化和零样本性能等多种设置；速度比同类方法快近100倍。

Conclusion: 该方法通过创新的位平面视角提供了简洁且高效的AI生成图像检测方案，在不增加复杂性的情况下显著提升了检测性能与速度，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of image generation models has made it increasingly difficult for people to distinguish AI-generated images from real ones. To prevent the potential risks associated with the misuse of fake images, AI-generated image detection has gained significant attention. Existing methods neglect the inherent differences between real and fake images, thus lacking robustness and generalization ability. In this work, we innovatively investigate AI-generated image detection using bit-planes, and introduce the bit-reversed image. We propose a simple yet effective pipeline consisting of construction of bit-reversed images, gradient-based patch selection and a convolutional classifier. Besides, we provide a theoretical analysis from the mathematical perspective to demonstrate the validity of our approach. We also introduce two challenging datasets for AI-generated image detection. Extensive experiments verify the effectiveness of our approach across different settings, including cross-generator generalization, cross-dataset generalization and zero-shot performance. Without bells and whistles, our approach outperforms existing methods on over 40 benchmarks, and is nearly 100 times faster than counterparts. The code is at https://github.com/renxi-seu/RAID.

</details>


### [18] [Classification of COVID-19 cases from chest CT volumes using hybrid model of 3D CNN and 3D MLP-Mixer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28978)
*Masahiro Oda,Tong Zheng,Yuichiro Hayashi,Yoshito Otake,Masahiro Hashimoto,Toshiaki Akashi,Shigeki Aoki,Kensaku Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的3D MLP-Mixer混合分类模型，用于COVID-19胸部CT体数据的自动分类。该方法结合3D CNN和3D版本的MLP-Mixer，利用局部和全局图像特征进行诊断辅助，在包含1205个CT体数据的数据集上达到79.5%的分类准确率，优于传统3D CNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19在全球范围内迅速传播，导致大量感染病例和死亡，医疗机构的医护人员严重短缺。对于包括COVID-19在内的病毒性肺炎病例，影像诊断需要同时关注肺部大面积的毛玻璃样混浊和实变影，因此局部和全局图像特征都很重要。传统的3D CNN模型难以充分捕捉全局特征，需要一种能够同时利用局部和全局特征的自动化分类方法，以实现高效的计算机辅助诊断。

Method: 该方法构建了一个混合分类模型，由3D卷积神经网络（CNN）和3D版本的MLP-Mixer组成。MLP-Mixer是一种基于Vision Transformer-like架构的图像分类方法，能够同时利用局部和全局图像特征。将MLP-Mixer扩展到3D版本以处理CT体数据，并与3D CNN相结合形成混合架构。

Result: 使用包含1205个CT体数据的数据集进行评估，所提出的方法达到了79.5%的分类准确率，高于由3D CNN层和简单MLP层构成的传统3D CNN模型。

Conclusion: 基于3D CNN与3D MLP-Mixer的混合模型能够有效结合局部和全局图像特征，在COVID-19胸部CT体数据分类任务上优于传统3D CNN模型，有望为临床提供可靠的计算机辅助诊断支持，缓解医疗人力不足的问题。

Abstract: This paper proposes an automated classification method of COVID-19 chest CT volumes using improved 3D MLP-Mixer. Novel coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) spreads over the world, causing a large number of infected patients and deaths. Sudden increase in the number of COVID-19 patients causes a manpower shortage in medical institutions. Computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) system provides quick and quantitative diagnosis results. CAD system for COVID-19 enables efficient diagnosis workflow and contributes to reduce such manpower shortage. In image-based diagnosis of viral pneumonia cases including COVID-19, both local and global image features are important because viral pneumonia cause many ground glass opacities and consolidations in large areas in the lung. This paper proposes an automated classification method of chest CT volumes for COVID-19 diagnosis assistance. MLP-Mixer is a recent method of image classification using Vision Transformer-like architecture. It performs classification using both local and global image features. To classify 3D CT volumes, we developed a hybrid classification model that consists of both a 3D convolutional neural network (CNN) and a 3D version of the MLP-Mixer. Classification accuracy of the proposed method was evaluated using a dataset that contains 1205 CT volumes and obtained 79.5% of classification accuracy. The accuracy was higher than that of conventional 3D CNN models consists of 3D CNN layers and simple MLP layers.

</details>


### [19] [Adjudicated Captioning: Multi-Agent Alignment Scoring and Consensus-Distilled Beam Arbitration for Strict Zero-Shot Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28986)
*Duy Tran Thanh,Thien-Phuc Doan,Long Nguyen-Vu,Ngo Tan Vu Khanh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Adjudicated Captioning，一个推理时的多智能体框架，在ICFCap标题生成器上通过三个检查点（更强的检索编码器、交叉注意力验证器、学习型重排序器）恢复视觉接地反馈，无需配对图像-标题监督或重新训练标题生成器，在COCO Karpathy上达到CIDEr 117.6，比IFCap提升+9.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本图像描述（ZIC）方法仅在检索阶段进行一次图像-文本对齐评分，之后解码器的自回归波束仅由语言模型概率驱动，缺乏持续的视觉接地反馈。自2024年以来没有方法在严格设置的基准上取得改进，进展停滞，因此需要一种能在多个检查点恢复接地反馈的推理框架。

Method: 在三个检查点干预：1) 输入侧安装更强的冻结检索编码器；2) 检索与解码之间插入冻结的交叉注意力验证器，将top-9检索重排为top-5；3) 输出波束上附加学习型重排序器，由TriFuse（MLP）和MemAttend（记忆注意力Transformer）组成，通过Borda-共识蒸馏在三个冻结评分器上自监督训练，不使用任何配对图像-标题标签或参考描述。

Result: 在归纳式无标签协议下，COCO Karpathy上CIDEr 117.6和SPICE 21.9，较IFCap的108.0和20.3提升+9.6 CIDEr；比最强合成图像增强方法NES（109.9）高出+7.7。非学习式架构干预贡献+7.8，学习型重排序器贡献+1.8。迁移到Flickr30k提升+8.1 CIDEr，NoCaps整体提升+5.7。

Conclusion: Adjudicated Captioning证明在不重新训练标题生成器、不使用任何配对图像-标题标签的前提下，通过推理时的多智能体架构干预就能显著提升零样本图像描述性能。非学习式架构改进贡献了大部分增益，而学习型重排序器通过自监督蒸馏进一步补充了少量提升，方法具有良好的跨数据集迁移性。

Abstract: Zero-shot image captioning (ZIC) describes images without paired image-caption supervision during captioner training, relying on text-only corpora and frozen pretrained image-text scorers. Existing retrieval-augmented methods score image-text alignment once, at retrieval, then commit the captioner's autoregressive beam under language-model probability alone, leaving the decoder without further visual grounding feedback. Progress has stalled, with no method improving on the strict-regime best since 2024.
  We propose Adjudicated Captioning, an inference-time multi-agent framework that restores grounding feedback at multiple checkpoints over an unchanged IFCap captioner. First, we install a stronger frozen Retrieval Encoder at the input. Second, between retrieval and decoding we insert a frozen Cross-Attention Verifier that re-ranks the top-9 retrievals to top-5. Third, at the output beam we attach a learned Reranker pairing TriFuse, a multilayer perceptron, with MemAttend, a memory-attended transformer, the pipeline's only learned components; both are trained self-supervised by Borda-consensus distillation across the three frozen scorers, using no paired image-caption labels and no reference captions.
  Under the inductive headline protocol, with rerankers fit on the disjoint COCO Karpathy validation beam and applied frozen to test, the framework reaches CIDEr 117.6 and SPICE 21.9 on COCO Karpathy, up from 108.0 and 20.3 for IFCap, a +9.6 CIDEr gain, and +7.7 above NES, the strongest synthetic-image-augmented method at 109.9, without retraining the captioner. A training-free fixed-fusion baseline reaches 115.8 CIDEr, so +7.8 of the +9.6 gain comes from the non-learned architectural intervention and the remaining +1.8 from the learned rerankers. The same recipe transfers off-COCO without captioner retraining: +8.1 CIDEr on Flickr30k Karpathy and +5.7 on NoCaps overall.

</details>


### [20] [CAER: Conflict-Aware Evidence Routing with Dual Prefix Experts for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28991)
*Zixuan Liu,Juntao Cai,Xiaoxu Cai,Haishuai Wang,Jiajun Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAER是一个与骨干网络无关的框架，通过跨度锚定的证据路由器实现细粒度冲突定位，并通过双前缀专家路由机制实现冲突感知生成，在不更新骨干参数的情况下提升多模态大语言模型在视觉-语言冲突场景下的可靠性与幻觉抑制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在文本输入与视觉证据冲突时仍会产生幻觉，输出与视觉内容不一致的响应。现有方法（解码策略、额外训练、验证方法、提示技术）缺乏细粒度的冲突定位和冲突感知生成能力。

Method: CAER包含两个核心组件：(1) 跨度锚定的证据路由器，将声明表示转化为软文本查询，并从冻结的视觉token中检索对应证据，实现细粒度冲突估计；(2) 双前缀专家路由机制，分别学习视觉支持和视觉矛盾的独立专家，通过显式专家选择实现冲突感知生成。

Result: 在公开的MMMC基准和作者新构建的AgriConflict数据集上，CAER在无需更新骨干参数的情况下，有效检测视觉-语言冲突，并提升了开源多模态大语言模型的可靠性。

Conclusion: CAER作为骨干无关框架，能够有效提升MLLMs在视觉-语言冲突场景下的表现，为减少多模态幻觉提供了一种可行的、无需修改骨干网络的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in multimodal understanding and generation. However, when textual inputs conflict with visual evidence, they still suffer from hallucinations and produce responses inconsistent with visual content. Existing approaches mainly rely on decoding strategies, additional training, verification methods, or prompting techniques, but often lack fine-grained conflict localization and conflict-aware generation. In this work, we propose CAER, a backbone-agnostic framework for visual-language conflict detection and conflict-aware generation. CAER introduces a span-grounded evidence router that transforms claim representations into soft textual queries and retrieves corresponding evidence from frozen visual tokens, enabling fine-grained conflict estimation. Furthermore, we design a dual-prefix expert routing mechanism that learns separate experts for visually supported and contradicted inputs, enabling conflict-aware generation through explicit expert selection. Experiments on the public MMMC benchmark and our newly curated AgriConflict dataset demonstrate that CAER effectively detects visual-language conflicts and improves the reliability of open-source MLLMs without updating their backbone parameters.

</details>


### [21] [SULAND v2: A Refined RGB Dataset and Deep Learning Object Detection Benchmark for UAV/UGV-Based SUrface LANDmine Detection Under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28996)
*Sagar Lekhak,Prasanna Reddy Pulakurthi,Lalit Joshi,Ramesh Bhatta,Emmett J. Ientilucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SULAND_v2，一个精炼后的RGB表面地雷检测数据集与基准。通过修复原SULAND数据集中的标注错误、定位误差、类别不一致等问题，并修正OOD类别ID约定，该数据集包含33,771张图像和12,433个边界框。在35种检测器配置上基准测试，标注精炼使YOLOv8的IID mAP@50提升14.6-19.6个百分点，修正OOD类别ID使OOD mAP@50提升约25个百分点。YOLOv12-Small在IID上最佳（0.908 mAP@50），RF-DETR-Large在OOD上最佳（0.799 mAP@50）。研究表明高IID精度不保证实际部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB图像为无人机/无人地面车辆在地表地雷探测中提供了实用且低成本的方案，但该安全关键领域中物体检测器研究不足，缺乏跨架构基准测试和OOD分析。现有SULAND数据集存在标注问题（缺失/错误标注、定位误差、可见性标准不一致、视觉伪影、时间标注不一致）及反转的OOD类别ID约定，阻碍了可靠的性能评估。

Method: 作者对SULAND数据集进行人工精炼，保留原始图像和划分，手工修订标注以确保完整性、精确定位、标签有效性和类别一致性，生成SULAND_v2。随后在九种检测器家族共35种配置上进行基准测试，包括IID和OOD评估。同时修复了OOD类别ID反转问题。

Result: 标注精炼使YOLOv8的IID测试mAP@50提升14.6-19.6个百分点；修复OOD类别ID约定使YOLOv8平均OOD mAP@50提升约25个百分点。在SULAND_v2上，YOLOv12-Small取得最佳IID性能（0.908 mAP@50），RF-DETR-Large取得最佳OOD性能（0.799 mAP@50, 0.675召回率）。

Conclusion: 高IID精度并不保证实际部署能力（OOD鲁棒性）。SULAND_v2为RGB地表排雷勘测支持中的域偏移鲁棒性评估提供了可靠基准，有助于推动该安全关键领域检测器的更深入研究。

Abstract: RGB imagery offers a practical, low-cost option for Unmanned Aerial/Ground Vehicle (UAV/UGV) survey support in surface-landmine detection, but object detectors remain underexplored in this safety-critical domain. Limited cross-architecture benchmarking and insufficient out-of-distribution (OOD) analysis obscure whether detectors generalize across deployment conditions. This challenge is amplified by the scarcity of public RGB landmine datasets, making SULAND a key benchmark for PFM-1 and PMA-2 detection. However, inspection reveals missing/false annotations, localization errors, inconsistent visibility criteria, visual artifacts, temporal labeling inconsistencies, and an inverted OOD class-ID convention in SULAND. We present SULAND_v2, a refined RGB surface-landmine dataset and benchmark. Preserving original images and splits, we manually revise annotations to ensure completeness, precise localization, label validity, and class consistency. SULAND_v2 contains 33,771 images and 12,433 bounding boxes. We benchmark 35 detector configurations across nine families. Annotation refinement improves YOLOv8 in-distribution (IID) test mAP@50 by 14.6-19.6 percentage points, while fixing the OOD class-ID convention increases mean YOLOv8 OOD mAP@50 by ~25 percentage points. On SULAND_v2, YOLOv12-Small achieves the highest IID mAP@50 (0.908), while RF-DETR-Large yields the strongest OOD performance (0.799 mAP@50, 0.675 recall). Our results demonstrate that high IID accuracy does not guarantee operational readiness. SULAND_v2 provides a reliable benchmark for evaluating domain-shift robustness in RGB-based mine-action survey support.

</details>


### [22] [Evaluation-Verification Reward for Consistent Multi-Reference Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29025)
*Yingmao Miao,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochen Lv,Meng Yu,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu,Chao Shen,Chenhao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多维评估-验证奖励模型(EVR),通过将多参考图像编辑的评价分解为多个视觉标准,并让MLLM评估器为每个标准生成多个候选假设,再由验证器基于具体视觉证据进行接受或拒绝,从而提供可靠且细粒度的奖励信号,使强化学习方法得以应用于多参考图像编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参考图像编辑面临两大挑战:一是缺乏能够捕获多图像关系约束的奖励模型,导致强化学习难以从单图像编辑扩展到多参考编辑;二是直接使用MLLM作为零样本评估器存在根本矛盾——长形式推理容易产生幻觉,而短形式判断的推理能力有限。

Method: 提出多维评估-验证奖励模型(EVR):将评价分解为多个视觉标准,每个标准由一个MLLM评估器生成多个候选假设,再由一个验证器将每个假设与具体视觉证据进行对照以接受或拒绝,最终产生可靠且细粒度的奖励信号。结合可扩展的数据管道,在无需修改架构的情况下对现成编辑器进行强化学习微调。

Result: 大量实验表明,该方法在Qwen-Image-Edit基础模型上取得了显著提升,在一致性和和谐性方面达到或超越了NanoBanana的性能。

Conclusion: EVR通过"评估-验证"分离的机制有效解决了MLLM评估器的幻觉与推理能力不足的矛盾,为多参考图像编辑的强化学习提供了可行的奖励建模方案,且无需改变编辑器架构即可显著提升编辑质量。

Abstract: While recent image editing models have made rapid progress, multi-reference editing remains challenging, particularly in maintaining visual consistency across references and ensuring overall visual harmony. Reinforcement learning has proven highly effective for text-to-image generation and single-image editing, but its extension to multi-reference editing is hindered by the absence of suitable reward models that capture multi-image relational constraints. Moreover, naively using multimodal large language models(MLLMs) as zero-shot evaluators faces a key tension between hallucination-prone long-form reasoning and the limited deductive power of short-form judgments. We address these issues with a Multi-dimensional Evaluation-Verification Reward(EVR). EVR decomposes evaluation into distinct visual criteria; for each criterion, an MLLM Evaluator generates multiple candidate hypotheses, and a Verifier grounds each claim in concrete visual evidence to accept or reject it, producing reliable and fine-grained reward signals. Together with a scalable data pipeline, our method enables RL fine-tuning of off-the-shelf editors without architectural changes. Extensive experiments show substantial gains over the base Qwen-Image-Edit, improving consistency and harmony to match or surpass NanoBanana.

</details>


### [23] [SAM+D: Parameter-Efficient Dimensional Lifting of SAM-Family Models via Depth-Routed LoRA and Depth Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29033)
*Yu Song,Hao Sun,Shiyu Teng,Ikuko Nishikawa,Yen-wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM+D是一个参数高效的框架，通过引入两个轻量级模块（DRLoRA和DSM），将2D的SAM/SAM2基础模型提升一个空间维度，实现3D体素分割和4D时空分割，仅需微调约2.8%-3.7%的参数即可在CT医学图像和细胞追踪等基准上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将2D基础模型（如SAM）适配到3D体积数据的方法存在两个缺陷：要么逐切片独立处理而忽略层间上下文信息，要么需要大量的架构改动和重新训练。作者旨在实现一个参数高效的提升方案，在不重建模型的前提下让SAM家族模型支持3D和4D分割任务。

Method: SAM+D在冻结的Transformer块中引入两个模型无关的轻量级模块：(1) Depth-Routed LoRA (DRLoRA)——带学习路由的空间自适应低秩更新专家模块；(2) Depth Shift Modules (DSM)——零参数代价的跨切片特征交换模块。这两个模块共同提供体级上下文，同时仅微调SAM约2.8%和SAM2约3.7%的参数。

Result: 在3D分割（SAM从2D提升到3D）中，在四个CT基准（KiTS, Pancreas, LiTS, Colon）上评估；在4D分割（SAM2从2D+T提升到3D+T）中，在细胞追踪挑战赛数据集（Fluo-N3DH-SIM+）上评估。两种设置下，SAM+D在单点提示（single-point prompt）下均取得有竞争力或更优的结果，同时使用的可训练参数少于现有方法。

Conclusion: SAM+D验证了其在不同SAM家族架构、目标维度（3D和4D）以及医学影像和生物场景理解等多个领域上的泛化能力，展示了参数高效微调在提升2D基础模型到高维空间的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Existing methods for adapting 2D foundation models such as SAM to 3D volumes either process slices independently---ignoring inter-slice context---or require substantial architectural changes and retraining. In this paper, we present \textbf{SAM+D}, a parameter-efficient framework that lifts SAM-family models by one spatial dimension---enabling 3D volumetric segmentation from 2D SAM and, for the first time via parameter-efficient fine-tuning, end-to-end 4D (3D+T) spatiotemporal segmentation from video-based SAM2---while keeping the vast majority of pre-trained parameters frozen. SAM+D introduces two lightweight, model-agnostic modules into frozen transformer blocks: (1)~\textbf{Depth-Routed LoRA (DRLoRA)} experts with learned routing for spatially adaptive low-rank updates, and (2)~\textbf{Depth Shift Modules (DSM)} for cross-slice feature exchange at zero additional parameter cost. Together, they provide volume-level context while tuning only ${\sim}$2.8\% of parameters for SAM and ${\sim}$3.7\% for SAM2. We evaluate SAM+D in two distinct settings, each lifting the base model by one spatial dimension: 3D segmentation, where SAM(2D$\,\to\,$3D) is evaluated on four CT benchmarks (KiTS, Pancreas, LiTS, Colon), and 4D segmentation, where SAM2 (2D+T$\,\to\,$3D+T) is evaluated on a cell tracking challenge (CTC) dataset (Fluo-N3DH-SIM+). In both settings SAM+D achieves competitive or superior results under the single-point prompt setting while using fewer trainable parameters than existing methods, demonstrating that SAM+D generalizes across SAM-family architectures, target dimensionalities (3D, 4D), and domains spanning medical imaging and bio-scene understanding. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/JerrySongCST/SAM-Plus-D.

</details>


### [24] [GO-PRE: Goal-Oriented Next-Best-View Selection via Predictive Rendering Entropy for Active 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29037)
*Yan Song,Zhihao Li,Chenglong Li,Li He,Yan Wang,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GO-PRE，一种目标导向的主动三维重建下一最佳视角选择框架，直接以预测空间的熵减少为目标，而非使用代理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动3D重建方法大多依赖代理信号（如参数不确定性或几何启发式），但这些信号与实际目标（渲染预测的保真度）不一致，导致捕获预算分配不够优化。

Method: GO-PRE将目标显式定义为在用户指定的目标视角流形上最大化平均边际预测熵的减少量。支持交互式目标指定，并推导出高效的采集规则，使信息增益可实时计算。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，GO-PRE相比最先进方法，在主动重建性能和不确定性量化可靠性方面均有持续改进。

Conclusion: 以预测空间信息增益为目标的视角选择策略，比基于代理信号的方法更能提升重建保真度，同时具备实时计算效率和交互灵活性。

Abstract: Active 3D reconstruction relies on active view selection to maximize reconstruction fidelity under limited capture budgets. However, most existing methods rely on surrogate signals such as parameter uncertainty or geometric heuristics, but these signals are often misaligned with the ultimate goal: the fidelity of rendered predictions. We propose GO-PRE, a goal-oriented next-best-view selection framework that explicitly targets information gain in the prediction space. Specifically, we formulate the objective as maximizing the reduction of the average marginal predictive entropy over a user-specified target view manifold. GO-PRE supports interactive goal specification and yields an efficient acquisition rule that enables real-time computation of information gain. Extensive experiments across benchmarks demonstrate that GO-PRE consistently improves active reconstruction performance and provides more reliable uncertainty quantification compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [25] [ReMoE: Report-Guided Mixture-of-Experts for Multimodal OCT/OCTA Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29039)
*Zihan Nie,Qincheng Qiao,Muhao Xu,Wei Feng,Xinguo Hou,Weiye Song,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReMoE框架，通过将正常医学报告的语义知识蒸馏到图像到文本的先验学生模型中，实现多模态视网膜OCT/OCTA异常检测，利用报告引导的混合专家路由进行特征调制，在私有数据集和公开OCTA500数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督视网膜OCT/OCTA异常检测方法仅依赖视觉特征分布、重建残差或编码器-解码器差异，异常分数只反映外观层面的偏差，忽略了多模态正常数据中由正常医学报告所描述的语义组织结构。作者希望将报告语义纳入异常检测以更全面地建模正常模式。

Method: 提出Report-Guided Mixture-of-Experts (ReMoE)，包含三个关键组件：(1) 将正常报告语义蒸馏到图像到文本的先验学生模型；(2) 构建模态感知的先验；(3) 使用报告引导的模态调制(Report-Guided Modality Modulation, RMM)通过混合专家路由来调制特征表达。

Result: 在私有OCT/OCTA配对正常报告数据集和公开OCTA500-3MM设置（使用固定正常报告）上均达到最先进的异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入正常报告语义作为多模态先验知识，能够超越仅基于视觉外观特征的异常检测方法，验证了语义级正常模式建模对医学多模态异常检测的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal medical anomaly detection identifies samples deviating from normal patterns, where scarce abnormal cases make normality modeling from normal data practical. In retinal Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT) and OCT Angiography (OCTA) anomaly detection, existing unsupervised methods rely on visual feature distributions, reconstruction residuals, or encoder-decoder discrepancies, making anomaly scores depend on appearance-level deviations, while multimodal normality also contains semantic organization described in normal medical reports. To this end, we propose Report-Guided Mixture-of-Experts (ReMoE), which distills normal report semantics into an image-to-text prior student, builds modality-aware priors, and uses Report-Guided Modality Modulation (RMM) to modulate features through mixture-of-experts routing. Experiments on a private OCT/OCTA dataset with paired normal reports and a public OCTA500-3MM setting using a fixed normal report demonstrate state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [26] [Rethinking Detection Calibration: A Coordinate and Direction Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29040)
*Juyong Lee,Seungjin Jung,Jungmin Lee,Sunju Lee,Jongwon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReDC框架，一种检测器后校准方法，为检测框提供坐标级别（含方向信息）的置信度分数，弥补现有校准方法仅基于框级定位的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度检测器常产生过度自信的预测，现有校准方法基于框级定位（如IoU或精度）校准置信度，但框级定位仅反映预测框与真实框的整体匹配程度，无法捕捉框内各坐标的定位精度，导致坐标级别的定位误差无法被置信度反映。

Method: 提出ReDC（Rethinking Detection Calibration）后校准框架，定义预测与真实值之间的坐标对齐度和偏差方向。基于对齐度进行置信度重编码，生成可靠的坐标级置信度分数；同时通过方向位移估计预测各坐标的偏差方向。此外，可将坐标级置信度聚合为框级定位结果。

Result: 在域内和跨域场景下的大量实验表明，ReDC能比现有方法更精确地表达检测对象的坐标级定位；同时通过聚合坐标级置信度可覆盖先前校准方法的表示范围。

Conclusion: ReDC提供包含方向信息的坐标级置信度校准，突破了传统框级校准的局限，能更细粒度地反映检测定位准确性。

Abstract: Deep learning based object detectors require trustworthiness beyond competitive detection performance, but deep neural networks are prone to overconfident predictions, assigning high confidence scores to predictions that are likely to be inaccurate. To improve the alignment between confidence scores and prediction accuracy, existing methods calibrate confidence scores based on box-level localization, such as precision or intersection over union with the ground truth bounding box. However, box-level localization reflects only a measure of agreement between the predicted box and the ground truth, resulting in calibrated confidence scores for box-level accuracy failing to capture the localization accuracy of coordinates of box. To tackle this issue, we propose a novel post-hoc calibration framework, rethinking detection calibration (ReDC), which provides reliable coordinate-level confidence scores, including directional information. The proposed framework defines coordinate-wise alignment and deviation direction between predictions and ground truth. Based on the alignment measure, confidence re-encoding produces reliable coordinate-level confidence scores, while directional displacement estimation predicts coordinate-wise deviation directions. Extensive experiments under in-domain and out-domain scenarios demonstrate that the proposed approach expresses the coordinate-wise localization of detected objects more precisely than existing methods. Furthermore, our method covers the representational scope of prior calibration approaches by aggregating coordinate-level confidence scores into box-level localization.

</details>


### [27] [Adaptive Emotional Video Captioning via Affective Heterogeneous Graph Reasoning and Multi-task Joint Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29045)
*Junbo Wang,Liangyu Fu,Yuke Li,Xuecheng Wu,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGML，一个通过软情感异构图和多任务语言建模的自适应情感视频描述（EVC）框架。SAGML用软情感异构图书符替代传统硬树形情感先验，通过软门控注入到视频到情感图的注意力中，使得词汇级情感在粗类别预测不准确时仍可恢复，同时支持混合和重叠情感的表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVC方法大多依赖全局情感向量或刚性分层先验。树形情感先验虽然建立了粗到细的连接，但其硬子节点掩码机制在粗类别预测错误时会不可逆地抑制正确的词汇级情感，且难以表达真实视频中常见的混合或重叠情感。这些问题促使了SAGML的提出。

Method: SAGML构建了一个包含目录级情感节点和词汇级情感词节点的软情感异构图书符。核心创新包括：（1）将软门控作为连续偏置注入视频到情感图的图注意力中，使视觉支持的词汇情感保持可恢复性而非被硬掩码删除；（2）将情感表示与视觉token共同输入因果语言解码器；（3）通过双目录/词汇头在提示隐藏状态上施加显式情感分布学习；（4）采用自回归字幕生成与情感分布监督的联合训练目标。

Result: SAGML提供了一个具有错误弹性和多情感感知能力的EVC基准。相比依赖硬掩码树形先验的方法，SAGML在粗类别预测不准确时仍能保留正确的词汇级情感，并能更好地处理真实视频中混合或重叠的情感。

Conclusion: SAGML通过软情感异构图和软门控机制有效解决了树形情感先验的硬掩码局限，在保持事实正确性的同时提升了情感表达力，为EVC任务提供了一个健壮且支持多情感感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Emotional video captioning (EVC) aims to describe a video with both factual correctness and affective expressiveness. It requires a model to perceive subtle, ambiguous, and temporally varying emotional cues and translate them into natural language without weakening objective visual content. Existing methods have progressively introduced contextual attention, emotion interpretation, emotion priors, dynamic emotion perception and emotion-cause reasoning. Nevertheless, most of them still depend on either global emotion vectors or rigid hierarchical priors. In recent methods, the tree-structured emotion prior establishes a coarse-to-fine connection between psychological emotion categories and daily emotion words, but its hard subordinate masking may irreversibly suppress correct lexical emotions once the coarse category prediction is inaccurate. It is also limited in representing mixed or overlapping emotions that frequently occur in real videos. To address the issues, we propose SAGML, an adaptive EVC framework via affective heterogeneous graph and multi-task language modeling. Instead of treating the emotion prior as a discrete tree, SAGML constructs a soft affective heterogeneous graph containing catalog-level emotion nodes and lexical-level emotion word nodes. The soft gate is injected into video-to-emotion graph attention as a continuous bias, allowing visually supported lexical emotions to remain recoverable rather than being removed by a hard mask. The resulting affective representation is fed together with visual tokens into a causal language decoder, while dual catalog and lexical heads impose explicit emotion distribution learning on the prompt hidden states. The overall model is trained with a joint objective that combines autoregressive caption generation and emotion distribution supervision. SAGML provides an error-resilient and multi-emotion-aware baseline for EVC.

</details>


### [28] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Spiking Point Cloud Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29048)
*Zihao Guo,Jihua Zhu,Yiding Sun,Lin Chen,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikePEFT，这是首个用于脉冲点云模型的参数高效微调框架。该方法通过内在动力学调优（IDT）和静默状态消歧适应（SSDA）两种策略，在仅更新约5%可训练参数的情况下，在ModelNet40和ScanObjectNN基准上分别达到92.4%和85.6%的准确率，同时保持SNN的能效优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练脉冲点云模型在下游任务适配时依赖全量微调，导致大量参数和存储开销；此外，二值脉冲传播会抑制与任务相关的亚阈值信息，限制了模型的适应性能。

Method: SpikePEFT包含两个关键组件：(1) 内在动力学调优（IDT）——自适应调节膜衰减和放电阈值，在冻结预训练突触变换的同时实现高效的神经元内在适应；(2) 静默状态消歧适应（SSDA）——从含有信息的静默状态中恢复任务相关信息，为下游适应提供更丰富的证据。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验证明了SpikePEFT的有效性和高效性。在ModelNet40上达到92.4%的准确率，在最具挑战性的ScanObjectNN(PB_T50_RS)分类分割上达到85.6%的准确率，仅更新约5%的可训练参数，同时保留了SNN的能效优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经形态视觉模型的参数高效适配迈出了有前景的一步，解决了全量微调开销大和脉冲传播信息丢失的问题。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer energy-efficient solutions for point cloud analysis on resource-constrained devices through event-driven computation. However, existing pre-trained spiking point cloud models rely on full fine-tuning for downstream task adaptation, incurring substantial parameter and storage overhead. Furthermore, binary spike propagation suppresses task-relevant sub-threshold information. To address these issues, we propose SpikePEFT, the first parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for spiking point cloud models. Specifically, Intrinsic Dynamics Tuning (IDT) adaptively modulates membrane decay and firing thresholds, enabling efficient neuron-intrinsic adaptation while keeping the pre-trained synaptic transformations frozen. Moreover, Silent-State Disambiguation Adaptation (SSDA) recovers task-relevant information from informative silent states, thereby providing richer evidence for downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of SpikePEFT. In particular, our method achieves 92.4% accuracy on ModelNet40 and 85.6\% on the most challenging classification split ScanObjectNN(PB\_T50\_RS) while updating only about 5% of the trainable parameters and preserving the energy efficiency of SNNs. This work provides a promising step toward parameter-efficient adaptation of neuromorphic vision models.

</details>


### [29] [Learning from Adversity: Semantic-Aware Mask Refinement through Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29059)
*Beomyoung Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Phoenix是一个基于对抗学习和对比学习的掩码细化框架，通过生成语义感知的噪声模式和建立三向对比学习框架，显著提升现有分割模型的掩码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分割模型即使是最先进的也会产生边界不完美、语义不一致和结构错误的掩码。掩码细化虽可解决这些问题，但当前方法依赖简单的合成噪声，无法捕捉真实分割模型的复杂错误模式。

Method: Phoenix框架包含两个核心创新：(1) 对抗掩码扰动（Adversarial Mask Perturbation），通过嵌入攻击生成语义感知的噪声以模拟真实分割错误；(2) 对比掩码细化学习（Contrastive Mask Refinement Learning），建立三向对比框架，确保语义区域内特征一致性同时保持类别间分离。

Result: 实验表明Phoenix在多种任务上显著优于现有方法，并能持续提升最先进分割模型的性能，带来实质性改进。

Conclusion: Phoenix通过对抗学习生成语义感知噪声并结合对比学习建模细化关系，有效解决了现有掩码细化方法中合成噪声不够真实的问题，为分割模型的掩码质量提升提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in image segmentation, even state-of-the-art models produce masks with imperfect boundaries, semantic inconsistencies, and structural errors. Mask refinement addresses these limitations, yet current approaches rely on simplistic synthetic noise that fails to capture the complex error patterns of real segmentation models. We introduce Phoenix, a novel framework that leverages adversarial learning to generate semantically meaningful noise patterns and contrastive learning to model refinement relationships. Our approach consists of two key innovations: (1) Adversarial Mask Perturbation, which employs embedding attacks to create semantic-aware noise that mimics real segmentation errors, and (2) Contrastive Mask Refinement Learning, which establishes a tri-directional framework that ensures feature consistency within semantic regions while maintaining separation between classes. Experiments demonstrate that Phoenix significantly outperforms existing methods across diverse tasks, while consistently enhancing state-of-the-art segmentation models with substantial improvements. Our code and project page are publicly available at https://phoenix-eccv26.github.io.

</details>


### [30] [MHRGait: Gait Recognition from Momentum Human Rig Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29083)
*Huiran Duan,Qian Zhou,Xianda Guo,Hua Zou,Guoying Zhao,Zhongyuan Wang,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将步态识别建立在紧凑的骨骼绑定控制参数上，而非传统的剪影、骨架或3D网格表示。作者引入MHR（Momentum Human Rig）姿态作为步态表征，并构建了MHRGait及其增强版MHRGait++，在四个基准数据集上证明了基于绑定空间（rig-space）的关节表征可作为独立有效的步态表示，并能与剪影互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别依赖三种输入表示：剪影（编码投影身体形状）、骨架（稀疏关节点）和3D网格（密集表面几何）。但这些表示中承载身份信息的关节运动都与服装、骨骼尺度或体型等几何载体耦合，难以解耦。作者质疑是否可以绕开几何载体，直接从紧凑的关节控制参数（articulated controls）中识别步态。

Method: 作者提出MHR姿态作为步态表示，从单目视频估计每帧184个按解剖学组织的身体和手部参数。MHRGait将这些异构控制参数按解剖分组，建模帧内协调性和时间演化，生成紧凑的身体与手部描述子。MHRGait++则通过模态平衡的距离融合将MHR姿态与剪影结合，避免描述子维度决定模态重要性。

Result: 在CCPG和SUSTech1K上，MHRGait在基于模型的方法中获得最佳整体性能并具有跨数据集迁移能力；其识别网络仅需2.76M参数和0.69 GFLOPs（30帧输入）。MHRGait++一致性地提升了剪影识别器的性能，实现了精度-效率的友好权衡。

Conclusion: 实验证明rig-space关节表征既可以作为独立有效的步态表示，也能作为投影身体形状的互补线索，为步态识别提供了新的输入表示范式。

Abstract: Gait recognition is shaped by its input representation. Silhouettes encode projected body shape, skeletons encode sparse joint coordinates, and 3D meshes encode dense surface geometry. In each case, identity-bearing articulation is observed through geometric carriers that also vary with clothing, skeletal scale, or body shape. We investigate whether gait can instead be recognized from compact articulated controls. We introduce Momentum Human Rig (MHR) pose as a gait representation, describing each frame using 184 semantically organized body and hand parameters estimated from monocular video. MHRGait groups these heterogeneous controls by anatomy, models their intra-frame coordination and temporal evolution, and produces compact body and hand descriptors. We further introduce MHRGait++, which combines MHR pose with silhouettes through modality-balanced distance fusion, preventing descriptor count from determining modality importance. Experiments on four benchmarks show that MHRGait attains the best overall performance among compared model-based methods on CCPG and SUSTech1K and transfers effectively across datasets, while its recognition network requires only 2.76M parameters and 0.69 GFLOPs for a 30-frame input. MHRGait++ consistently improves silhouette recognizers with a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. These results establish rig-space articulation as an effective standalone gait representation and a complementary cue to projected body shape. Our code is available at https://github.com/duanhuiran/MHRGait.

</details>


### [31] [Forwardrobe: Garment-Aware Gaussian Avatars from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29106)
*Daisheng Jin,Shuyun Wang,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Forwardrobe是一个从单张图像重建可动画化3D人体头像的前馈框架，通过将服装与身体在规范高斯空间中显式分离，并引入连续性感知的几何与蒙皮初始化、姿态条件非刚性变形和外观自适应，解决了宽松衣物（如裙子、连衣裙）的几何与运动表示难题，同时支持服装编辑、迁移和3D虚拟试穿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像重建可动画化的3D人体头像对宽松衣物尤其困难，因为宽松衣物的几何和运动无法被与身体对齐的拓扑结构和蒙皮合理表示。现有方法在这一场景下表现不佳。

Method: Forwardrobe采用前馈（feed-forward）框架，核心设计包括：(1) 在规范高斯空间中将服装与身体显式分离；(2) 为服装层配备连续性感知的几何与蒙皮初始化；(3) 姿态条件的非刚性形变；(4) 外观自适应。

Result: 实验表明，与现有单图像头像重建方法相比，Forwardrobe在服装重建质量和动画过程中的视觉质量上均有提升，尤其在裙子和连衣裙场景下效果显著。此外，分离的服装层形成了独立可控的3D资产，支持服装编辑、迁移和3D虚拟试穿。

Conclusion: 通过将服装分层处理并配备定制化的形变与外观设计，Forwardrobe有效改善了宽松衣物的重建与动画质量，同时提供了比现有方法更灵活的服装操作能力，展现了在虚拟试穿等下游应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Reconstructing animatable 3D human avatars from a single image remains particularly challenging for loose garments, whose geometry and motion cannot be adequately represented by body-aligned topology and skinning. We present Forwardrobe, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing garment-aware Gaussian avatars from a single image. Forwardrobe explicitly separates clothing from the body in canonical Gaussian space and equips the garment layer with continuity-aware geometry and skinning initialization, pose-conditioned non-rigid deformation, and appearance adaptation. These designs improve garment reconstruction and visual quality during animation, particularly for skirts and dresses. The separated garment layer additionally forms an independently controllable 3D asset, enabling garment editing, transfer, and 3D virtual try-on. Experiments demonstrate improved garment reconstruction quality and greater flexibility in garment manipulation compared with existing single-image avatar reconstruction methods.

</details>


### [32] [A Frozen Pixel-Space Diffusion Model Can Guide Itself with Its Own Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29122)
*Zixuan Fu,Chong Wang,Lanqing Guo,Kailai Zhou,Jiahao Nie,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出合成自引导（SSG）方法，利用冻结的预训练像素扩散模型内部中间层的粗预测与最终层细预测之间的差异作为自引导方向，在不重新训练主模型的前提下改善生成质量。训练辅助头仅需生成样本而非真实图像，计算量不到全模型训练的1%，在ImageNet上使FID降低超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型需要在同一高维空间同时捕捉全局结构和局部纹理，这具有挑战性。现有改进方案（如替代预测目标、训练目标和架构）通常需要从零训练新模型，成本高昂。作者希望找到一种更廉价、互补的策略——利用冻结的预训练像素扩散模型本身来自我引导，无需重新训练主模型。

Method: 关键观察：预训练像素扩散Transformer的中间层可以解码为捕捉低频结构的粗预测，而最终层逐步细化局部高频细节。方法：（1）在中间层附加轻量预测头，保持骨干网络冻结；（2）利用中间层与最终层预测之间的差异作为采样期间的自引导方向；（3）训练该预测头时无需真实图像，使用模型自身的生成样本即可，且生成样本在增强像素扩散欠拟合的高频分量方面优于真实图像。

Result: 在ImageNet上多个像素扩散模型（JiT、PixelREPA等变体）上，SSG一致改善生成质量：在不使用分类器自由引导（CFG）的设置下FID降低超过50%；在强基线（已含CFG）上进一步改进，如JiT-H/16从1.86降至1.67，PixelREPA-H/16从1.81降至1.59。适配头训练计算量不到全模型训练的1%。

Conclusion: 冻结的预训练像素扩散模型可以通过中间层与最终层的预测差异实现自我引导，从而在不重新训练主模型的情况下显著提升生成质量。使用合成数据训练辅助头不仅可行而且优于真实图像，尤其在改善高频细节方面。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models aim to learn an end-to-end generator directly over raw pixels. This is challenging because a single model must capture both global structure and local texture in the same high-dimensional space. While recent work improves pixel diffusion through alternative prediction targets, training objectives, and architectures, these advances typically require training a new model from scratch. We show there is a cheaper, complementary strategy: \textbf{a frozen, pretrained pixel diffusion model can guide itself}. Our key observation is that intermediate layers of a pretrained pixel diffusion transformer can be decoded into coarse predictions that capture the main low-frequency structure, while the final layers progressively refine local, high-frequency details. We therefore attach a lightweight prediction head to an intermediate layer, keep the backbone frozen, and use the discrepancy between the intermediate and final predictions as a self-guidance direction during sampling. To train this head, we further find that real images are not necessary. Instead, model-generated samples suffice and even outperform real images for training the head, especially in enhancing the high-frequency components that pixel diffusion tends to underfit. Across multiple pixel diffusion models on ImageNet, our \textbf{Synthetic Self-Guidance (SSG)} consistently improves generation while adapter training requires less than 1$\%$ of full-model training compute: it reduces FID by over 50$\%$ across the evaluated JiT variants without classifier-free guidance (CFG) and further improves strong baselines with CFG, e.g., JiT-H/16 from 1.86 to 1.67 and PixelREPA-H/16 from 1.81 to 1.59. Our code is available at https://github.com/zfu006/SSG.

</details>


### [33] [SciFigPlag-Bench: A Benchmark for Provenance-Aware Scientific Figure Plagiarism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29124)
*Zhiying Cui,Minghao Yang,Linlin Gao,Jie Liu,Pengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigPlag-Bench，一个用于科学图表抄袭检测与溯源推理的基准数据集，包含2,582个正样本对和2,541个负样本对，支持四个诊断任务，并揭示现有视觉语言模型在细粒度溯源推理中的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表编码了研究背后的视觉证据，但图表抄袭作为一个多模态评估问题尚未被充分探索。已有的图像相似性检测或图像取证基准无法回答"可疑图表是否复用了特定来源图表的证据、如何被变换、以及复用的证据出现在哪里"这类溯源推理问题。

Method: 引入一个因子化分类法，将"复用什么"与"如何变换"分离，涵盖材料保留型复用（整图复用、子图复用）和抽象内容复用（数据重表达、结构重绘）。在此基础上构建混合基准，结合文献记录的真实案例、分类法指导的合成样本以及视觉相似负样本，构建2,582个正样本对和2,541个负样本对，并定义四个诊断任务：成对检测、来源归属、层级化复用类型分类、复用对应区域定位。

Result: 在多种视觉语言模型上建立了初始基线，实验揭示出现有模型在细粒度溯源推理、复用类型理解以及空间证据定位方面仍存在持续挑战。

Conclusion: SciFigPlag-Bench为科学图表抄袭检测提供了一个系统化、可诊断的评估框架，填补了该领域基准缺失的空白，同时表明当前视觉语言模型在此任务上仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: Scientific figures often encode the visual evidence behind scientific findings, yet figure plagiarism remains underexplored as a benchmarked multimodal evaluation problem. We present SciFigPlag-Bench, a benchmark for provenance-aware reasoning over scientific figures in scholarly documents. Unlike general image-similarity or image-forensics benchmarks, SciFigPlag-Bench evaluates whether a suspicious figure reuses evidence from a specific source figure, how the reused content has been transformed, and where the reused evidence appears. We introduce a factorized taxonomy that separates what is reused from how it is transformed, covering material-preserving reuse, such as full-figure and subfigure reuse, as well as abstract-content reuse, such as data re-expression and structural redraw. Guided by this taxonomy, we construct a hybrid benchmark with 2,582 positive pairs and 2,541 negative pairs, combining documented real-world cases, taxonomy-guided synthetic examples, and visually similar negatives. The benchmark supports four diagnostic tasks: pairwise detection, source attribution, hierarchical reuse-type classification, and reuse correspondence localization. Experiments with diverse vision-language models establish initial baselines and reveal persistent challenges in fine-grained provenance reasoning, reuse-type understanding, and spatial evidence grounding.

</details>


### [34] [First Investigation of Deep Learning for Intraoperative Gauze Segmentation in Minimally Invasive Abdominal Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29132)
